TARGET is always 4R+') → D-R1 W0 ratification 2026-09-04 → operator re-confirmed 2026-09-16 ('i said before 2.5 is an acceptable minimum'). ANY code site enforcing 3.0 is superseded slop. ### R10 — pressure-speed-exit

## MECHANISMS — the 583 extracted laws (the overhaul)

### active-sl-trigger-030

> "Any LSZ within 0.30 DXY points of current spot"

Near-spot sweep zones arm stop-hunt entries and SL cascades; distant LSZ stays informational. Stale flags invite Asian-session fills. v4.0 -- check 4.5 supersession.

### anchor-construction-token

> "\"Set E at the TP level of trade #1, SL 3 pips outside the far edge of the nested liquidity zone…"

Anchors chain Law-27 style (TP of N = entry of N+1) with SL at 3p outside the back edge or 1-2p inside the next-rung close edge, TP at the strength-driven opposing edge, and the RRR ≥2.5 gate applied last as consequence. Fabricated non-zone levels throw under E3-NEVER.

## TERMS — vocabulary

### BoM

Break-of-Momentum: the zone born AT the break = the ORIGIN. Unmitigated + prior NONE/OPPOSITE → the 100% reaction zone, the highest-conviction anchor.

**↔ Connections:** isolated (cross-link pass pending)


### FORTRESS

4+ strong holds (defense events) → the Fire→Earth transition → ~95% probability the next move FLIPS at that zone. The strongest single E1 signal.

**↔ Connections:** isolated (cross-link pass pending)


### MoM

Momentum: the zone born DURING the move. A VIRGIN MoM mid-BS/WL pathway = the DEATH ZONE (the one exception to the 3-mitigation rule).

**↔ Connections:** isolated (cross-link pass pending)


### OBCV

Order-Block Confirmation Vision gates: Displacement (body ≥1.5× avg AND range ≥1.5× ATR) · Freshness (bw ≥3.0 AND bf ≥0.65) · Temporal (a 30m orphan inside the OB → REJECT).

**↔ Connections:** isolated (cross-link pass pending)


### PHANTOM_BOM

Type-2 virgin MoM: large-OB + fakeout + pre-formation opposing-BoM reaction + macro-origin continuation (all-4 predicate). Classified at the formation window; the FORTRESS exemption to the death rule.

**↔ Connections:** isolated (cross-link pass pending)
